It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"103:1","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":9,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":14,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"103:1","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"103:1","surface_ref":"103:1","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"103:1","target_tokens":[["Zamana",["103:1:1"]],["yemin",["103:1:1"]],["olsun",["103:1:1"]]],"text":"Zamana yemin olsun."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":9,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":3,"id":"s103-p01-001-003","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["103:1","103:2","103:3"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_119d049eb526f27d2c23","text":"The later ayat pushed `العصر` away from a single time-gloss. 103:2’s `خسر` activated the press/accounting readings (`ع ص ر B002`, `ع ص ر B007`) because loss looked like extracted deficit. 103:3’s repeated mutual `و ص ي` and final `صبر` then made `ع ص ر B005` unexpectedly central: time is not only what consumes, but the condition in which refuge and clinging become legible. The cloud/rain reading remained exploratory, but the basmalah’s `س م و B004` plus `ص ب ر B010` made it more than a loose image.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B002","root_001019/B005","root_001019/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_342aa4319329896fc216","text":"Ayah 103:2 first activated the press-and-ledger mechanism by placing خ س ر `B002` immediately after the oath: pressure could now expose an unprofitable yield. Ayah 103:3 then corrected a purely destructive reading of ع ص ر. Its security, joining, work, and mutual surety made ع ص ر `B005` visible as refuge and `B007` as extracted gain; its sound acts also made the rain-release image of `B003` newly coherent, though that material analogy remains exploratory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B005","root_001019/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40a21334016bb12427c1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:1:1\",\"root_001019\",[\"B005\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Aynı sözcük, insanı sıkıştıran koşulu da bu koşul içinde başkalarına tutunarak bulunan sığınağı da çağrıştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40ccc7834820008fef35","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:1:1\",\"root_001019\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Zamanın baskısı, sıkışan buluttan yağmur çıkması gibi, uygun insani eylemden yarar doğurabilen bir süreç olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B002","root_001019/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_60dbfdefe2f78d2e601c","text":"2. **A press that exposes yield or deficit.** **Lexical evidence:** ع ص ر `B002` (pressing until liquid runs) and `B007` (extracted yield or gain), activated by خ س ر `B002` (trade loss), ع م ل `B001` (intentional work), and ع م ل `B004` (wage for work). **Structural cue and role:** pressure is named first, commercial deficit next, and labor-like corrective outputs last; the press supplies the process, human action the input, and sound deeds and counsel the possible yield. **Abductive move:** the causal arrow “time-pressure extracts what a life actually produces” is inferred from this order and branch convergence. **Reading change:** the oath invokes an assay-like process: under pressure, the human either yields a return or is disclosed as consuming capital without profit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_001019/B005","root_001019/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6490cb8bb4ad4a1e3acb","text":"3. **Withholding and rescue under scarcity.** Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B005, \"refuge, rescue, and clinging,\" and B006, \"withholding, holding back, and reclaiming\"; activated by `ء م ن` B001, secure trust, `و ص ي` B003, mutual exhortation, and `ص ب ر` B001/B003, self-restraint and suretyship. Structural cue: 103:3 is introduced by `إلا`, making a marked exit from the condition in 103:2. Assigned role: `العصر` names the constricted situation in which rescue requires attachment and disciplined holding. The packet supplies the exception and the branches of clinging, security, reciprocal charge, and restraint. I infer that the exception functions as a refuge-route out of the constriction. Reading change: the ayah can be heard as swearing by the pressure-zone in which humans are held back or rescued by what they cling to together.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B002","root_001019/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_742b2fff6b01fe53802f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:1:1\",\"root_001019\",[\"B002\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Zaman, insan ömrünü sıkarak onun gerçek ürününü ortaya çıkaran bir basınç sınamasına dönüşür: sonuç ya kazanç ya da eksilmedir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000840/B010","root_001019/B003","root_001019/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a715f0b4aef876fb97c8","text":"4. **A weather-and-growth cycle.** Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B003, \"rain-clouds squeezed into rain,\" and B010, \"grain enclosed in its husks\"; activated by `ص ب ر` B010, \"white layered cloud,\" `و ص ي` B004, pasture suiting grazing animals, and `ص ل ح` B003, suitability or fitness. Structural cue: the last ayah supplies ecological branches clustered around cloud, pasture, fitness, and endurance after the oath-root already offers rain-bearing clouds. Assigned role: `العصر` is the season-like compression that brings rain and encloses grain until maturity. The packet supplies all the ecological images; I supply the arrow that rain/season enables fit growth. Reading change: the oath can open a latent agricultural reading: human loss is set against a natural process in which pressure, enclosure, and patient suitability are conditions of fruitfulness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B001","root_001019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bbc0494ce7c2d63e9762","text":"1. **Time as the container that exposes loss.** Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B001, \"age or recurring time\"; activated by `خ س ر` B001/B002 in 103:2 and by the fourfold exception in 103:3. Structural cue: the oath precedes a spatial verdict, \"in loss,\" then an exception. Assigned role: `العصر` supplies the enveloping duration in which the human condition becomes measurable. The packet supplies: time → human → loss → exception. I supply the directional arrow: recurring time is the medium that lets deficiency show itself. Reading change: the ayah is not only \"by time\" as a neutral swear-word; it becomes an oath by the very temporal field in which human value is depleted unless a counter-pattern appears.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000840/B008","root_001019/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c63f4bdddad6ebb7d8be","text":"Later 103:3 made the refuge branch of `ع ص ر` B005 more visible than it first appeared: `تواصوا` twice does not only advise, it creates a clinging network. `ص ب ر` B008, bitter extract, also echoed back onto `ع ص ر` B002: the pressure may produce not sweetness but medicine. Finally, `ح ق ق` B011, fitted socket, and `ص ب ر` B018, stopper or plug, retrospectively made `العصر` feel less like open flowing time and more like a constrained apparatus in which things are held, pressed, and made to fit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d9b1bf15b19f1b2adffd","text":"4. **Pressure can release life-giving rain.** **Lexical evidence:** ع ص ر `B003` (rain-clouds squeezed into rain), activated by س م و `B004` (overhead cloud and rain), ر ح م `B001` (beneficence), and ص ل ح `B001` (soundness and doing good). **Structural cue and role:** the opening supplies an overhead mercy-frame, the oath supplies compressed rain-bearing material, and the exception supplies beneficial human output. **Abductive move:** exploratorily, I treat rain released from pressure as a material analogue for sound action released from temporal pressure; only the images and sequence, not that analogy, are packet-supplied. **Reading change:** the oath need not image pressure as purely destructive: it can also name the condition under which withheld benefit is released.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000840/B010","root_001019/B001","root_001019/B002","root_001019/B003","root_001019/B005","root_001046/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dccbea0b7534d2781c3f","text":"1. Time as the recurring witness of human attrition. Lexical evidence: fixed `ع ص ر B001` (age, recurring time, day/night, afternoon), activated by `ء ن س B001` (visible human presence) and `خ س ر B001` (general deficiency). Structural cue: the isolated oath-noun is followed by an emphatic universal clause about the human. Assigned role: `العصر` supplies the temporal field in which the human condition is observed. Abductive move: I supply the evidentiary arrow, that recurring time is not only the object sworn by but the witness that exposes deficiency. Reading change: the ayah reads less as “time is important” and more as “the repeated passage of time visibly proves the human’s diminishing condition.” 2. Time as a press that extracts either loss or yield. Lexical evidence: fixed `ع ص ر B002` (pressing until liquid runs) and `ع ص ر B007` (giving, yield, extracted gain), activated by `خ س ر B002` (trade loss), `ع م ل B004` (wage/pay for work), and `ع م ل B005` (transactions between people). Structural cue: the sequence moves from `العصر` to `خسر` and then to acts, works, and mutual obligations. Assigned role: `العصر` becomes the press; human life and work are the material pressed; loss or gain is the extracted result. Abductive move: I supply the economic-material conversion, that the later vocabulary of loss/work lets the pressure image become an accounting of yield. Reading change: the oath becomes a pressure-test: time squeezes human existence until its real profit, deficiency, or waste appears. 3. Time as constriction that also names the needed refuge. Lexical evidence: fixed `ع ص ر B005` (refuge, rescue, clinging) and `ع ص ر B006` (withholding/holding back), activated by `و ص ي B001` (joining one thing to another), `و ص ي B003` (mutual exhortation), and `ص ب ر B001` (self-restraint from panic). Structural cue: a terse oath is followed by being “in” loss, then by an exception formed through collective attachment. Assigned role: `العصر` supplies both the compressive danger and the lexical possibility of resort. Abductive move: I infer that the same pressure that exposes loss also forces the search for a clinging-place, and that the exception describes that place socially. Reading change: `العصر` no longer names neutral duration; it names a crisis-space in which rescue requires attachment, not escape from time. 4. A rain-cloud/irrigation reading of the oath. Lexical evidence: fixed `ع ص ر B003` (rain-clouds squeezed into rain), activated by basmalah `س م و B004` (overhead sky/cover, cloud, rain), `ر ح م B001` (beneficence), `ص ل ح B003` (suitability/fitness), and `ص ب ر B010` (white layered cloud). Structural cue: the opening has an overhead divine-merciful frame, and the closing lists practices that can make human ground receptive and sound. Assigned role: `العصر` is the rain-bearing compression, while faith/work/right counsel/patience are the cultivated receptivity that can receive yield rather than loss. Abductive move: I supply the ecological arrow, that mercy from above and pressure in the cloud can become beneficial only where the human field is made fit. Reading change: the oath can be heard as more than late time: it also evokes compressed cloud and rain, making the surah’s rescue-pattern agricultural and formative.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e16fbe4fd7017bdacbc2","text":"3. **The sworn term contains its own refuge branch.** **Lexical evidence:** ع ص ر `B005` (refuge, rescue, and clinging), activated by ء م ن `B001` (security), و ص ي `B001` (joining), and ص ب ر `B003` (standing with or guaranteeing another). **Structural cue and role:** `إلا` opens an escape from the enclosing `في خسر`, and the exception is plural and reciprocal; the ع ص ر branch supplies the refuge-image, while trust, joining, and solidarity construct it. **Abductive move:** I infer that 103:3 instantiates the latent refuge in the noun sworn by; the packet does not explicitly equate the exception with `العصرة`. **Reading change:** `والعصر` can signal both the condition that presses humanity and the possibility of clinging rescue within that condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B002","root_001019/B002","root_001019/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e65a192fa42e2b85f24f","text":"2. **The press that extracts the hidden yield.** Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B002, \"pressing until liquid runs,\" and B007, \"giving, yield, and extracted gain\"; activated by `خ س ر` B002, \"trade loss,\" and by `ع م ل` B001/B004/B005 in 103:3, work, wage, and transaction. Structural cue: 103:1 is a compact noun, then 103:2 names loss, then 103:3 names practices. Assigned role: `العصر` becomes the press; human life is the material put under pressure; faith, work, truth, and patience are the extractable yield. The packet supplies the press-branch, the loss-branch, and the work/transaction branches. I infer that pressure reveals whether a yield or a deficit results. Reading change: the oath points to a compressive test, not merely duration; what comes after it is what runs out when existence is squeezed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f67a673e50bf991f6b8f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:1:1\",\"root_001019\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan zaman, insanın azalışını görünür kılan ve davranışlarının yönünü sürekli kaydeden bir tanık gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fdbc5f38e23bfde8a05b","text":"1. **Recurring time as an accumulating ledger.** **Lexical evidence:** ع ص ر `B001` (age or recurring time). **Structural cue and role:** the sworn noun precedes the emphatic generalization of 103:2, while 103:3 answers with a coordinated sequence of intentional and repeatedly reciprocal acts; recurring time is the medium in which both depletion and repair accumulate. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies succession and repeated forms, while I infer that recurrence makes omissions and corrective acts compound. **Reading change:** `العصر` denotes not merely a date or late hour but the succession that continuously registers the human trajectory.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001019/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ffd2bb7a396c4cd0bba5","text":"5. **The choking morsel and the small saving sip.** Lexical evidence: `ع ص ر` B008, \"sipping to get a choking morsel down\"; activated by `ص ب ر` B001, restraint, and `و ص ي` B003, repeated mutual advising. Structural cue: the exception is not one grand act but a sequence of held practices, with `تواصوا` repeated. Assigned role: `العصر` becomes the acute constriction, the stuck point; the later virtues act like repeated small sips that allow passage. The packet supplies the choking/sipping branch and repeated mutual counsel. I infer that repetition turns the rescue into gradual relief rather than instant escape. Reading change: the ayah’s compactness can be read as the throat-tight moment before the surah releases a measured remedy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
